### Q47. JEE Main 2022 (25 Jul Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

<p>While performing a thermodynamics experiment, a student made the following observations, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>HCl</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>NaOH</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>NaCl</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi>&#916;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>57</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>COOH</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>NaOH</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>COONa</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#916;H</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>55</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>.</p><p>The enthalpy of ionization of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>COOH</mi></math> as calculated by the student is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>kJ</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>. </p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **2**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>The enthalpy of neutralization (&Delta;H<sub>n</sub>) is the change in enthalpy that occurs when one equivalent of an acid and a base undergo a neutralization reaction to form water and a salt. It is a special case of the enthalpy of reaction. It is defined as the energy released with the formation of 1 mole of water.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mo>&#9651;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>Ionization</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>of</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>weak</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>acid</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>=</mo><msub><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>Neutralization</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>of</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">W</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8722;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>Neutralization</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>of</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>=</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>&#916;H</mi><mi>ionisation</mi></msub></math> of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>COOH</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>55</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>57</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>KJ</mi><mo>/</mo><mi>mol</mi></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>KJ</mi><mo>/</mo><mi>mol</mi></math> of heat is absorbed for the ionization of acetic acid.</p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q49. JEE Main 2022 (29 Jun Shift 1)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>17</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math> completely vapourises at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>33</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>42</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#176;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math> bar pressure and the enthalpy change in the process is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>23</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>. The enthalpy change for the vapourisation of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>85</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math> under the same conditions is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>kJ</mi></math>.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **117**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>17</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mn>17</mn><mn>17</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mole</mi></math> and the enthalpy change = <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>23</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>.</p><p> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>85</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mn>85</mn><mn>17</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>moles</mi></math> enthalpy change under the same conditions will be = <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>23</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>117</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi></math>.</p>

</details>

### Q50. JEE Main 2022 (26 Jun Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> fish swimming in water body when taken out from the water body is covered with a film of water of weight <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>36</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math>. When it is subjected to cooking at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>100</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#176;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>, then the internal energy for vaporization in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>kJmol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> is integer]</p><p>[Assume steam to be an ideal gas. Given <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi>vap</mi></msub><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>&#8854;</mo></msup></math> for water at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>373</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math> bar is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>41</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>:</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>31</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>]</p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **38**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Weight of water on fish <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>36</mn></math> gram</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mfenced><munderover><mo>&#8594;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mi>bar</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>100</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#176;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></munderover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>;</mo><msub><mi>&#916;H</mi><mi>vap</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>41</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mole</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#916;H</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#916;U</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>&#916;n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></msub><mi>RT</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>41</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>&#916;U</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>314</mn></mrow><mn>1000</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mn>373</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#916;U</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>82</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>76</mn><mi>kJ</mi></math></p><p>so change in internal energy <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>in</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><mo>/</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>76</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>38</mn><mi>kJ</mi><mo>/</mo><mi>mole</mi></math></p>

</details>

### Q51. JEE Main 2022 (26 Jun Shift 1)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

<p>For complete combustion of methanol</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>OH</mi><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><msub><mi>CO</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mfenced></math> the amount of heat produced as measured by bomb calorimeter is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>726</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>27</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#176;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>. The enthalpy of combustion for the reaction is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>, where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math> is
integer)
(Given : <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>JK</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>)</p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **727**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>OH</mi><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><msub><mi>CO</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mfenced><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>For chemical reaction : <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#916;H</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#916;U</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>&#916;n</mi><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced></msub><mi>RT</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#916;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>726</mn><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mn>1</mn></mstyle><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mn>8</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>314</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>300</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>727</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>245</mn><mi>KJ</mi><mo>/</mo><mi>mole</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>727</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q52. JEE Main 2022 (25 Jun Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `1`

At <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>25</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#176;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>atm</mi></math> pressure, the enthalpy of combustion of benzene <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math> and acetylene <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced></math> are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>3268</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>1300</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>, respectively. The change in enthalpy for the reaction <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>6</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>6</mn></msub><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mfenced></math>, is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>+</mo><mn>324</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>+</mo><mn>632</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>632</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>732</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>632</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Standard enthalpy of combustion is defined as the enthalpy change when one mole of a compound is completely burnt in oxygen with all the reactants and products in their standard state under standard conditions (298K and 1 bar pressure)</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>&#916;H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mfenced><msub><mi>&#916;H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></msub></mfenced><mi>reactant</mi></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mfenced><msub><mi>&#916;H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></msub></mfenced><mi>product</mi></msub></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><msub><mi>&#916;Hc</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></msub><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><msub><mi>&#916;H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></msub><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>6</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>6</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1300</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3268</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3900</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3268</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>632</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><mo>/</mo><mi>mole</mi></math></p>

</details>

### Q56. JEE Main 2021 (27 Jul Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

<p>When <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>400</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mL</mi></math> of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi>SO</mi><mn>4</mn></msub></math> solution is mixed with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>600</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mL</mi></math> of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>1</mn><mi>MNaOH</mi></math> solution, the increase in temperature of the final solution is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8212;</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math>. (Round off to the nearest integer).</p><p>[Use :<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>+</mo></msup><mo>(</mo><mi>aq</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>OH</mi><mo>-</mo></msup><mo>(</mo><mi>aq</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8594;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>:</mo></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open=""><mrow><msub><mi>&#916;</mi><mi>&#947;</mi></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>57</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math> </p><p>Specific heat of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>18</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> , density of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>cm</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math> </p><p>Assume no change in volume of solution on mixing.</p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **82**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>+</mo></msup></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>400</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>1000</mn></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>16</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><msup><mi>OH</mi><mo>-</mo></msup></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>600</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1000</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>06</mn><mo>(</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>Limiting</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>Reagent</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>Now we can apply, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>ms</mi><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math></p><p> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>06</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>57</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>3</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>1000</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>18</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mi>&#916;T</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>&#916;T</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>8196</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>81</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>96</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>&#8776;</mo><mn>82</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math></p>

</details>

### Q57. JEE Main 2021 (27 Jul Shift 1)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

For water at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>100</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math> bar,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>&#916;</mi><mrow><mi>v</mi><mi>a</mi><mi>p</mi><mo>&#160;</mo></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>&#916;</mi><mrow><mi>v</mi><mi>a</mi><mi>p</mi><mo>&#160;</mo></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">U</mi><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow></mrow><mo>&#175;</mo></munder><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
(Round off to the Nearest Integer)
[Use : <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open=""><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>31</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
[Assume volume of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is much smaller than volume of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></math>. Assume <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></math> treated as an ideal gas]

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **31**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#8467;</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></msub><mo>&#8652;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></msub></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#916;H</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#916;U</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>&#916;n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></msub><mi>RT</mi></math>
for <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math> mole waters ; <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>&#916;n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>&#916;n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></msub><mi>RT</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>31</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi><mo>/</mo><mi>mol</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>373</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>3099</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>63</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi><mo>&#8773;</mo><mn>31</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>

</details>

### Q58. JEE Main 2021 (25 Jul Shift 1)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `1`

At <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>298</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math>, the enthalpy of fusion of a solid <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></mfenced></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> and the enthalpy of vaporisation of the liquid <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></mfenced></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>98</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>. The enthalpy of sublimation of the substance <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></mfenced></math> in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>kJ</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> is ______. (in nearest integer)

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **101**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>&#916;H</mi><mi>sub</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>&#916;H</mi><mrow><mi>fus</mi><mo>.</mo></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>&#916;H</mi><mrow><mi>vap</mi><mo>.</mo></mrow></msub></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>98</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>101</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><mo>/</mo><mi>mol</mi></math></p>

</details>

### Q59. JEE Main 2021 (25 Jul Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `1`

At <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>298</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math> the relationship between enthalpy of bond dissociation (in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>kJ</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>) for hydrogen <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></msub></mfenced></math> and its isotope, deuterium <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></msub></mfenced></math>, is best described by:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></msub></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></msub></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></msub></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Enthalpy of bond dissociation <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>kJ</mi><mo>/</mo><mi>mole</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>298</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math></p><p>For, hydrogen <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>435</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>88</mn></math></p><p>For, Deuterium <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>443</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>35</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></msub><mo>&#8771;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p>

</details>

(Use : <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>&#916;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></math> (glucose)<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2700</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>)

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>10000</mn><mn>2700</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>7037</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>moles</mi></math>
wt. of glucose <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>7037</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>180</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>666</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>666</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8776;</mo><mn>667</mn><mi>gm</mi></math></p><p> </p>

</details>

### Q63. JEE Main 2021 (17 Mar Shift 1)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

<p>The standard enthalpies of formation of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>Al</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>CaO</mi></math> are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>1675</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>635</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> respectively.</p><p>For the reaction</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>CaO</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>Al</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>Ca</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>Al</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math> the standard reaction enthalpy <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></msub><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>0</mn></msup><mo>=</mo></math> ________ <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>kJ</mi><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>(Round off to the Nearest Integer).</p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **230**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given reaction:</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>CaO</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>Al</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msub><mi>Al</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>Ca</mi></math></p><p>Now, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>&#176;</mo><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>&#931;&#916;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></msub><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><msub><mrow/><mtext>Products&#160;</mtext></msub><mo>&#186;</mo></msubsup><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>&#931;&#916;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></msub><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi>Reactants</mi><mo>&#186;</mo></msubsup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>[</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1675</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>]</mo><mo>-</mo><mo>[</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>635</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>]</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>230</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math></p>

</details>

### Q65. JEE Main 2021 (25 Feb Shift 1)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

The ionization enthalpy of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>Na</mi><mo>+</mo></msup></math> formation from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>Na</mi><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced></msub></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>495</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo></math> while the electron gain enthalpy of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Br</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>325</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>. Given the lattice enthalpy of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>NaBr</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>728</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> . The energy for the formation of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>NaBr</mi></math> ionic solid is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mo>-</mo></mfenced><mi>_</mi><mi>_</mi><mi>_</mi><mi>_</mi><mi>_</mi><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **5576**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/1dd4623b-54d3-4a91-8224-af605fbf3a86-image.png" style="width: 367px; height: 153px;" /></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>&#916;H</mi><mrow><mi>formation</mi><mo>&#160;</mo></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>IE</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>&#916;Heg</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>LE</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>495</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>325</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>728</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>557</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>6</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5576</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi>KJ</mi><mo>/</mo><mi>mol</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>Note: The above calculation is not for</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>&#916;H</mi><mrow><mi>formation</mi><mo>&#160;</mo></mrow></msub></math> but for <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>&#916;H</mi><mrow><mi>Reaction</mi><mo>&#160;</mo></mrow></msub></math></p><p>But on the basis of given data it is the best ans.</p>

</details>

### Q66. JEE Main 2021 (25 Feb Shift 1)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `1`

The reaction of cyanamide, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi>CN</mi><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mfenced></msub></math> with oxygen was run in a bomb calorimeter and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#916;U</mi></math> was found to be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>742</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>24</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> . The magnitude of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>&#916;H</mi><mn>298</mn></msub></math> for the reaction  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi>CN</mi><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mfenced></msub><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced></mrow></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mfenced></msub></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>kJ</mi></math>. (Rounded off to the nearest integer) [Assume ideal gases and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>314</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>]

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **741**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#916;H</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#916;U</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>&#916;n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></msub><mi>RT</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>742</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>24</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>314</mn></mrow><mn>1000</mn></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>298</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>741</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><mo>/</mo><mi>mol</mi></math></p><p>Hence answer is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>741</mn></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

### Q67. JEE Main 2020 (05 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

Lattice enthalpy and enthalpy of solution of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>NaCl</mi></math> are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>788</mn><msup><mi>kJmol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>and  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mi>k</mi><mi>J</mi><mi>m</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>l</mi><msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>, respectively. The hydration enthalpy of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>N</mi><mi>a</mi><mi>C</mi><mi>l</mi><mo>&#160;</mo></math>is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>780</mn><msup><mi>kJmol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>780</mn><msup><mi>kJmol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>784</mn><msup><mi>kJmol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>784</mn><msup><mi>kJmol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>784</mn><msup><mi>kJmol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Enthalpy of solution= Lattice enthalpy + enthalpy of hydration</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>788</mn><mo>+</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8710;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi>hydration</mi></msub><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#8710;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi>hydration</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>784</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>KJ</mi><mo>/</mo><mi>mol</mi></math></p>

</details>

### Q68. JEE Main 2020 (02 Sep Shift 1)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

<p>The internal energy change (in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>) when <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>90</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> of water undergoes complete evaporation at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>100</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> is ..............</p><p>(Given : <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>&#916;H</mi><mi>vap</mi></msub></math> for water at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>373</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>41</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><mo>/</mo><mi>mol</mi></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>314</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>JK</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>)</p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **189494**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Number of moles of water =<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>90</mn><mn>18</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>moles</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>&#916;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#916;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">U</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#916;</mi><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></msub><mi>RT</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>41000</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>&#916;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">U</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>314</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>373</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>205000</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>&#916;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">U</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>15505</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>61</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#916;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">U</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>189494</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>39</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>189494</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math></p>

</details>

### Q69. JEE Main 2020 (09 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `1`

If enthalpy of atomization for <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x&#160;</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mrow></math> and bond enthalpy for <math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> is <math><mi mathvariant="normal">y&#160;</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mrow></math>, the relation between them

**Options:**

- **(A)** is <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></math>.
- **(B)** does not exist.
- **(C)** is <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></math>.
- **(D)** is <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></math>.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) is <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></math>.**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/31cf960e-87c8-4248-91c3-0c58892879c9.jpeg" />
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8710;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi>atomisation</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8710;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi>Vap</mi></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>Bond</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>E</mo><mi>nergy</mi></math>
Hence <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></math></p><p>Enthalpy of atomization is enthalpy change involved when one mole of gaseous atom is formed from a substance in an elemental state (Elemental state of Bromine is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>Br</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mo>)</mo></math>).</p><p>While, bond energy is energy required to break one mole of bonds in gaseous state to form separated atoms.</p>

</details>

### Q70. JEE Main 2020 (07 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

The standard heat of formation <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mo>&#8710;</mo></mrow><mrow><mi>f</mi></mrow></msub><msubsup><mrow><mi>H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>298</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msubsup></mrow></mfenced></math> of ethane (in <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mrow></math> ), if the heat of combustion of ethane, hydrogen and graphite are <math><mo>-</mo><mn>1560</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>393.5</mn></math> and <math><mo>-</mo><mn>286&#160;</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mrow></math> , respectively is ________

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **-192**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mtext>C</mtext><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mtext> graphite </mtext><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mtext>g</mtext><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mo>→</mo><msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mn>6</mn> </msub> <mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mtext>g</mtext><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mi>Δ</mi> <mtext>f</mtext> </msub> <mtext>H</mtext><mfenced> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mn>6</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>Δ</mi><msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>comb </mtext></mrow> </msub> <mfenced> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>gaphite </mtext></mrow> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>Δ</mi><msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>comb </mtext></mrow> </msub> <mfenced> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mi>Δ</mi><msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>comb </mtext></mrow> </msub> <mfenced> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mn>6</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mo>−</mo><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mn>286</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>2</mn><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mo>−</mo><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mn>393.5</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>3</mn><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mo>−</mo><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>1560</mn><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>572</mn><mo>−</mo><mn>1180.5</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1560</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>192.5 </mn><mtext>kJ</mtext><mo>/</mo><mtext>mole</mtext></mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q71. JEE Main 2019 (12 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

Enthalpy of sublimation of iodine is <math><mn>24</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mn>200</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> . If specific heat of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> (s) and <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> (vap) are <math><mn>0.055</mn></math> and <math><mn>0.031</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> respectively, then enthalpy of sublimation of iodine at <math><mn>250</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> in <math><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>11.4</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2.85</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>22.8</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>5.7</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>22.8</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>250</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>200</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>250</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>24</mn><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0.013</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0.055</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>50</mn></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>250</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>24</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1.2</mn></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>250</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>22.8</mn><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q72. JEE Main 2019 (10 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

The difference between <math><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></math> and <math><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">U</mi></math> is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>-</mo><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">U</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>, when the combustion of one mole of heptane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mfenced></math> is carried out at a temperature <math><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math> , is equal to:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Combustion of heptane

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>11</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#10230;</mo><mn>7</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">U</mi><mo>+</mo><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>

<math><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>-</mo><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">U</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>

<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>

</details>

### Q73. JEE Main 2019 (08 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

For silver, <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>23</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0.01</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>.</mo></math> If the temperature <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> of <math><mn>3</mn></math> moles of silver is raised from <math><mn>300</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mn>1000</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math> pressure, the value of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></math> will be close to:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>16</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>62</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>13</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>21</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>62</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mo stretchy="true">&#8747;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></msubsup><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msubsup><mo stretchy="true">&#8747;</mo><mn>300</mn><mn>1000</mn></msubsup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>23</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0.01</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>23</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1000</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>300</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.01</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>1000</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mn>300</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>61950</mn><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi><mo>&#8776;</mo><mn>62</mn><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi><mo>.</mo></math>

</details>

### Q74. JEE Main 2019 (12 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

Given:

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mfenced></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>C</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mi>graphite</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><msub><mi>CO</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>;</mo></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>&#916;rH</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#920;</mi></msup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>kJ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mi>ii</mi></mfenced></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>graphite</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>CO</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>&#160;;</mo></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>&#916;rH</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#920;</mi></msup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>kJ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mi>iii</mi></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>CO</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><msub><mi>CO</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>;</mo></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>&#916;rH</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#920;</mi></msup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>kJ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

Based on the above thermochemical equations, find out which one of the following algebraic relationships is correct?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo><msub><mi>CO</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>,<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>&#916;H</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math>
We get the above equation by adding <math><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math> and ( <math><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>CO</mi></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mi>&#916;H</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>CO</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo><msub><mi>CO</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>            <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#916;H</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo><msub><mi>CO</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>                  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#916;H</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></math>

</details>

### Q75. JEE Main 2018 (08 Apr)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

The combustion of benzene <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mfenced></math> gives <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>CO</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mfenced><mo>.</mo></math> Given that heat of combustion of benzene at constant volume is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>3263</mn><mo>.9</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>kJ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>25</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>;</mo></math> the heat of combustion <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>in</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>kJ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math> of benzene at constant pressure will be

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>.314</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi>JK</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>3267.6</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>4152.6</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>452.46</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>3260</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mo>-</mo><mn>3267.6</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>6</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>6</mn></msub><msub><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mfenced><mrow></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>15</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mrow></mrow></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>6</mn><msub><mi>CO</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><msub><mo>)</mo><mrow></mrow></msub><mo>&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#8756;&#160;&#160;</mo><mo>&#8710;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>

Use, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#916;H</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#916;U</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>&#916;n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></msub><mi>RT</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3263.5</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>8.314</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>298</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#160;</mi></math>

 =<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>3267</mn><mo>.6</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>kJ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math></p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q76. JEE Main 2017 (08 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

The enthalpy change on freezing of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></math> of water at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> to ice at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> is:

(Given <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi>fus</mi></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>kJ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>,</mo></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>75</mn><mo>.3</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>36</mn><mo>.8</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> )

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mo>.00</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>kJ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo>.81</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>kJ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo>.44</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>kJ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mo>.56</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>kJ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mo>.56</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>kJ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>In order to calculate the enthalpy change for <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math> at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> to ice at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>,</mo></math> we need to calculate the enthalpy change of all the transformation involved in the process.</p><p>(a) Energy change of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced><mi>l</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mi>l</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math></p><p>(b) Energy change of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi><mo>,</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mi>l</mi></mfenced></math> at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mi>ice</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math></p><p>(c) Energy change of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></math>, Ice <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mfenced></math>, at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi><mo>,</mo></math> Ice <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math></p><p>Total <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#916;H</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></msub><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#916;T</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#916;H</mi></math> freezing <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>+</mo></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></msub><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#916;T</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>75</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>)</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>36</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>)</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#916;H</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>56</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> (exothermic process)</p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#916;H</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>56</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kJ</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>.</p>
